~~~python
        pairs.append((sign * v, abs(host.act(ww, probe)[readout] - e0)))
        k3 &= sign * host.step(ww, wcond)[x] >= sign * v
    pairs.sort()
    k2 = bool(pairs) and pairs[0][1] > 0 and all(pairs[i][1] < pairs[i + 1][1] for i in range(len(pairs) - 1))
    return dict(lb=lb, st=st, tri=tri, K2=k2, K3=k3, cert=(tri == 'Trace-Response carrier' and k2 and k3))


def macro_cleared(host, w, e, r, dist=None):
    """Continued-treatment macro-step: event beat → r rest beats → clear the mouth bar of path P (m_P := 0) → at most one perturbation (frozen order)."""
    w = host.step(w, e)
    for _ in range(r):
        w = host.step(w, 'R')
    w = dict(w, mP=ZERO)
    return apply_dist(host, w, dist)

def rel_basin(theta, j='P'):
    """Relation with the actual sediment delivery to the lake as target quantity: the path carries the main throughflow on event beats, and its sediment input to the lake basin is > 0 with a share ≥ θ of the total lake-basin input over all paths."""
    o = 'Q' if j == 'P' else 'P'
    def rel(host, w, e):
        _sig, t, y = host.terms(w, e)
        if y['Q' + j] < theta * host.ev[e][0]:
            return False
        a, b = t[j]['basin'], t[o]['basin']
        return a > 0 and a >= theta * (a + b)
    return rel

def replay_hist_path(host, macro_states, macro_events, t, W, theta, conds=('B', 'S'), nmin=2, halves=2):
    """Class-labeled path reading Ω^c of Ω_W (path and effective history; main text 8.6):
    (1) re-traversal records: event k is of type c, R* holds when read along path P (gully mouth → channel reach → mouth of P), and the previous same-type event in the window also held along the same path (repetition;
         a single above-threshold event counts only as a candidate event); at least nmin records;
    (2) Brk_W = 0: before every macro-step in the window and at t, the actual conductance g_P of P is > 0, and at every event beat in the window the actual throughflow Q_P of P is > 0 (the route edge is in effect);
    (3) the window is divided into halves equal segments, and every segment has a re-traversal record of c. 1 only if this holds for every c in conds. Return (value, item by item, candidates and records)."""
    if t < W:
        return 0, {}, {}
    rel = rel_main(theta)
    ks = list(range(t - W, t))
    cand = {c: [k for k in ks if macro_events[k] == c and rel(host, macro_states[k], c)] for c in conds}
    recs = {}
    for c in conds:
        same = [k for k in ks if macro_events[k] == c]
        recs[c] = [k for k in cand[c] if same.index(k) > 0 and same[same.index(k) - 1] in cand[c]]
    brk = any(host.cond(macro_states[k], 'P') <= 0 for k in ks + [t]) or \
        any(host.act(macro_states[k], macro_events[k])['QP'] <= 0 for k in ks)
    seg = W // halves
    out = {}
    for c in conds:
        f1 = len(recs[c]) >= nmin
        f3 = all(any(t - W + i * seg <= k < t - W + (i + 1) * seg for k in recs[c]) for i in range(halves))
        out[c] = (f1, not brk, f3)
    v = int(all(all(x) for x in out.values()))
    return v, out, {'candidates': cand, 'records': recs}
~~~

A.2  dm21\_lake.py  Extended model 𝔐21⁺: lake level, lake-outlet outflow channel, channel-depth memory, new crevasse channel, clamp arm, duty criteria frozen in advance

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program: the river–lake feedback and avulsion extension model 𝔐21⁺ (for the local baseline 𝔐21 see dm21_model.py).
Standard library only; every number is a fractions.Fraction; no randomness. Interface as in [17] Appendix A.1 jhc_core.Host; booking, clipping and gridding within a beat are carried out by jhc_core.Host.step.
On top of 𝔐21 it adds:
  1. A third, potential route N (a new crevasse channel on the floodplain): existence variable eN ∈ {0, 1} ([18] D18-12); until it is generated it carries neither read-out nor writing;
  1′. Every route records the maximum incision depth x_j its channel reach has attained (channel-depth memory; during rest it shallows at the same rate as c_j), used in the crevasse criterion for in-channel aggradation;
      the new channel N is a shorter path: its conductance is divided by the length factor lenN, and its channel-reach slope is S0N (slope advantage);
  2. Lake-basin storage and release: the lake water volume WL and the lake-basin sediment V jointly set the lake level ℓ = WL/AL + kV·V (basin infilling raises the base level);
      the lake level enters the channel-reach backwater of every existing route (S_c = (S0 − (m_j + ℓ)/Lb)⁺), i.e., at least two water routes read the lake-basin state;
  3. Lake-outlet channel D (a further water route): outflow QD = ko·(ℓ − zo)⁺, zo = z0 − cD (outlet incision lowers the spillway sill); the incision cD of D is written by above-threshold scour;
  4. Avulsion (crevassing): when the in-channel aggradation x_j − c_j of an existing route's channel reach reaches Hs times its channel depth h0 + x_j and the inflow is a high flow, N is generated if it has not been yet: eN := 1, and u_N, c_N take the initial cut depth d0;
      healing of the abandoned route is left to rest diffusion (κ);
  5. Clamp arm of the lake-basin feedback: with clamp = True the lake level is held at ℓ₀ (WL and V are booked as usual but enter neither reading nor writing), and outflow is still computed at ℓ₀.
  6. Outflow is split into “potential outflow” outflow_pot(w) = ko·(ℓ − zo)⁺ and “realized outflow” QD = min(potential, available water);
      available water: WL + Q (same-beat inflow) on an event beat, WL on a rest beat; no outflow on a freeze-thaw beat. Outlet incision and the water account are always written from the realized outflow;
      rest-beat evaporation is min(ev·WL, WL − QD) (evaporation never exceeds the water left after outflow), so the lake water volume is never cut off by clipping of a requested amount;
      with ample remaining water (QD ≤ (1 − ev)·WL) evaporation is exactly ev·WL.
  7. The route-P sediment-routing switches betaP, exportP are wired in with the same meaning as in 𝔐21 (the same split as dm21_model.Geo.path_terms):
      betaP is the mouth-bar retention rate of route P; if exportP is true, the part not retained on the bar, (β − betaP)·out, is exported from the system; if false, it is reassigned to the lake basin.
The forms and sources of the mechanism laws are registered in main text 11.2.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import jhc_core as JH
import dm21_model as G0
ZERO = Fr(0)
ROUTES = ('P', 'Q', 'N')
SITES = ('uP', 'cP', 'mP', 'uQ', 'cQ', 'mQ', 'uN', 'cN', 'mN', 'eN', 'xP', 'xQ', 'xN', 'h', 'V', 'WL', 'cD')
pos = G0.pos
XBASE = dict(G0.BASE)
XBASE.update(AL=Fr(8), kV=Fr(1, 32), ko=Fr(1, 2), z0=Fr(1, 2), SD=Fr(3, 4), keD=Fr(1, 4), wcD=Fr(1, 8), ev=Fr(1, 16),
             Hs=Fr(3, 10), d0=Fr(1, 4), lenN=Fr(1, 2), S0N=Fr(3, 2), WLmax=Fr(64), cDmax=Fr(1, 2), clamp=False, ell0=ZERO, avulse=True)

class GeoX(JH.Host):
    name = 'dm21_lake'
    def __init__(self, par=None, events=None, init=None):
        self.p = dict(XBASE)
        if par:
            self.p.update(par)
        self.ev = dict(G0.EVENTS if events is None else events)
        p = self.p
        self.X = list(SITES)
        self.lo = {x: ZERO for x in SITES}
        self.hi = {'h': p['hmax'], 'V': p['Vmax'], 'WL': p['WLmax'], 'cD': p['cDmax'], 'eN': Fr(1)}
        for j in ROUTES:
            self.lo['u' + j] = self.lo['c' + j] = p['lo']
            self.hi['u' + j] = self.hi['c' + j] = self.hi['x' + j] = p['hi']
            self.hi['m' + j] = p['mhi']
        self.ground = {x: ZERO for x in SITES}
        self.orient = {x: 1 for x in SITES}
        base = dict(G0.INIT)
        base.update(uN=ZERO, cN=ZERO, mN=ZERO, eN=ZERO, xP=ZERO, xQ=ZERO, xN=ZERO, WL=ZERO, cD=ZERO)
        self.init = dict(base if init is None else init)
        self.grid = p['G']
    # ---- Read-outs of the lake-basin state
    def level(self, w):
        p = self.p
        if p['clamp']:
            return p['ell0']
        return w['WL'] / p['AL'] + p['kV'] * w['V']
    def outflow_pot(self, w):
        """Potential outflow (spill request): ko·(ℓ − (z0 − c_D))⁺."""
        p = self.p
        return p['ko'] * pos(self.level(w) - (p['z0'] - w['cD']))
    def outflow(self, w, s='R'):
        """Realized outflow: potential outflow capped by the available water (WL + Q on an event beat, WL on a rest beat, 0 on a freeze-thaw beat)."""
        if s in self.ev:
            return min(self.outflow_pot(w), w['WL'] + self.ev[s][0])
        if s in ('R', 'rest'):
            return min(self.outflow_pot(w), w['WL'])
        return ZERO
    def exists(self, w, j):
        return j != 'N' or w['eN'] == 1
    def cond(self, w, j):
        if not self.exists(w, j) or j in self.p.get('closed', ()):
            return ZERO
        a = self.p['g0'] * (self.p['h0'] + w['u' + j]) ** 2
        b = self.p['g0'] * (self.p['h0'] + w['c' + j]) ** 2
        g = a * b / (a + b)
        return g / self.p['lenN'] if j == 'N' else g
    def route(self, w, Q):
        """Kirchhoff flow division over n parallel routes, each with outlet sill height m_j and no backflow (successively dropping routes whose sill height is not below the nodal head)."""
        act = [j for j in ROUTES if self.cond(w, j) > 0]
        while act:
            gs = sum(self.cond(w, j) for j in act)
            phi = (Q + sum(self.cond(w, j) * w['m' + j] for j in act)) / gs
            drop = [j for j in act if phi - w['m' + j] <= 0]
            if not drop:
                return {j: (self.cond(w, j) * (phi - w['m' + j]) if j in act else ZERO) for j in ROUTES}
            act = [j for j in act if j not in drop]
        return {j: ZERO for j in ROUTES}
    def pj(self, j):
        """Parameters of route j: the new channel N takes channel-reach slope S0N (slope advantage of the shorter path); the others as in 𝔐21."""
        return dict(self.p, S0=self.p['S0N']) if j == 'N' else self.p
    def supply(self, w, s):
        return min(w['h'], self.ev[s][1])
    def terms(self, w, s):
        Q, _smax, _lev = self.ev[s]
        qs = self.route(w, Q)
        sig = self.supply(w, s)
        ell = self.level(w)
        t = {}
        for j in ROUTES:
            if not self.exists(w, j):
                continue
            tj = G0.pterms(self.pj(j), qs[j], Q, sig, w['m' + j], ell)
            tj['export'] = ZERO
            if j == 'P' and 'betaP' in self.p:          # same split as dm21_model.Geo.path_terms
                bP, b = self.p['betaP'], self.p['beta']
                tj['mouth'] = bP * tj['out']
                if self.p.get('exportP', False):
                    tj['basin'] = (1 - b) * tj['out']
                    tj['export'] = (b - bP) * tj['out']
                else:
                    tj['basin'] = (1 - bP) * tj['out']
            t[j] = tj
        return sig, t, qs
    def act(self, w, s):
        y = {'Q' + j: ZERO for j in ROUTES}
        y.update({'S' + j: ZERO for j in ROUTES})
        y['QD'] = self.outflow(w, s)
        if s not in self.ev:
            return y
        sig, t, qs = self.terms(w, s)
        for j in ROUTES:
            y['Q' + j] = qs[j]
            y['S' + j] = t[j]['out'] if j in t else ZERO
        return y
    def avulsion_due(self, w, s):
        """Crevasse criterion (read before the event beat): high flow B arrives, N has not been generated, and the in-channel aggradation of some existing route reaches threshold: x_j − c_j ≥ Hs·(h0 + x_j)
        (x_j is the maximum incision depth the route's channel reach has attained and h0 + x_j its channel depth; in-channel aggradation reaching Hs times the channel depth meets the crevasse condition). Returns the parent route or None."""
        p = self.p
        if not p['avulse'] or s != 'B' or w['eN'] == 1:
            return None
        for j in ('P', 'Q'):
            if w['x' + j] - w['c' + j] >= p['Hs'] * (p['h0'] + w['x' + j]):
                return j
        return None
    def comps(self, w, y, s):
        p = self.p
        out = []
        QD = y['QD']
        if s in ('R', 'rest'):
            for j in ROUTES:
                if self.exists(w, j):
                    out += [('diff', 'u' + j, -p['kap'] * w['u' + j]), ('diff', 'c' + j, -p['kap'] * w['c' + j]),
                            ('diff', 'x' + j, -p['kap'] * w['x' + j]), ('rework', 'm' + j, -p['mu'] * w['m' + j])]
            out += [('weather', 'h', p['wd']), ('outflow', 'WL', -QD), ('evap', 'WL', -min(p['ev'] * w['WL'], w['WL'] - QD)),
                    ('outcut', 'cD', p['keD'] * pos(QD * p['SD'] - p['wcD'])), ('diff', 'cD', -p['kap'] * w['cD'])]
            return out
        if s == 'F':
            return [('frost', 'h', p['ft'])]
        Q, _smax, _lev = self.ev[s]
        sig, t, qs = self.terms(w, s)
        out.append(('strip', 'h', -sig))
        for j in t:
            tj = t[j]
            out += [('scour_e', 'u' + j, tj['du']), ('scour_c', 'c' + j, tj['dc']),
                    ('mouth', 'm' + j, tj['mouth']), ('basin', 'V', tj['basin']),
                    ('deepest', 'x' + j, pos(w['c' + j] + tj['dc'] - w['x' + j]))]
        out += [('inflow', 'WL', Q), ('outflow', 'WL', -QD), ('outcut', 'cD', p['keD'] * pos(QD * p['SD'] - p['wcD']))]
        mother = self.avulsion_due(w, s)
        if mother is not None:
            out += [('avulse', 'eN', Fr(1)), ('avulse', 'uN', p['d0'] - w['uN']), ('avulse', 'cN', p['d0'] - w['cN']),
                    ('avulse', 'xN', p['d0'] - w['xN'])]
        return out

def run(host, w, hist):
    return JH.run(host, w, hist)

def macro(host, w, e, r):
    w = host.step(w, e)
    for _ in range(r):
        w = host.step(w, 'R')
    return w

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Duty criteria frozen in advance (main text 11.4)
def role_holder(host, w, theta, metric='out'):
    """Duty ρ*: a route carries the main throughflow (share ≥ θ) under both inflow classes and carries the main sediment flux (its sediment flux > 0 and share ≥ θ).
    Sediment-flux metric: 'out' is channel-exit sediment (default, the frozen criterion), 'basin' is the sediment actually delivered to the lake basin. Returns the set of bearers."""
    out = []
    for j in ROUTES:
        if not host.exists(w, j):
            continue
        ok = True
        for e in ('B', 'S'):
            sig, t, qs = host.terms(w, e)
            Q = host.ev[e][0]
            oj = t[j][metric]
            ot = sum(t[k][metric] for k in t)
            if not (qs[j] >= theta * Q and oj > 0 and oj >= theta * ot):
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            out.append(j)
    return out

def same_kind(host, w):
    """Same-kind relation (weaker): a route takes the majority of throughflow (share > 1/2) under both inflow classes. Returns the set of such routes."""
    out = []
    for j in ROUTES:
        if host.exists(w, j) and all(host.route(w, host.ev[e][0])[j] * 2 > host.ev[e][0] for e in ('B', 'S')):
            out.append(j)
    return out

def classify(holders0, holders, kinds):
    """Along the actual trajectory, registers the current status against the initial duty bearers holders0 (declared in advance):
    'original path kept': an original bearer still bears the duty; 'new path bears same duty': no original bearer bears it, but another route does;
    'same-kind relation only': no route bears ρ*, but some route takes the majority of throughflow; 'duty interrupted': neither."""
    if any(j in holders for j in holders0):
        return 'original path kept'
    if holders:
        return 'new path bears same duty'
    if kinds:
        return 'same-kind relation only'
    return 'duty interrupted'

def majority(host, w, e, j='N'):
    """Whether route j takes more than half of the actual flow division under inflow e (Q_j > Q/2)."""
    return host.route(w, host.ev[e][0])[j] * 2 > host.ev[e][0]
~~~

A.3  dm21\_law.py  Model-side reading of law-formation read-back: Established, DynamicRole, ΩSupport (segment-level Ω\_W) and macro-step-by-macro-step read-back

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program: law-formation read-back of one candidate relation (model side; local model 𝔐21, fixed topology).
Candidate law L*: Rel(L*) = R*_θ(P) (θ = 2/3): when event i arrives, route P carries the main throughflow of the hillslope inflow (Q_P ≥ θQ_i) and the main channel-exit sediment transport
(S_P > 0 and S_P ≥ θ(S_P + S_Q)). Applicable coverage domain I = I_cov = {B, S}. Carrying organization R = {u_P, c_P}. Window W = 8 macro-steps (ending at t).
All of the above is frozen before computation (main text 8.5).
This module gives, item by item, the model-side reading of the structural predicates of the main book's Law Chapters (for the matching declarations see main text 8.5; for “proved / proxy / semantic condition / gap” see Section 14):
  Established (*The General Form of Law*, Section 2): CurrentCarrier ∧ CarrierRealizes (coverage) ∧ CarrierNonInstant (coverage) ∧ sedimentary carrying (the [17] three-way split gives Trace-Response carrier);
  DynamicRole (Section 3, existential polarity): there exist i ∈ I and a legitimate counterfactual value of R (other positions fixed) such that the duty's actual activity (Q_P, S_P of event i)
      and its write-back (Δu_P, Δc_P) both change; whether the read-out R*_θ(P) flips is reported separately (a stronger witness);
  ΩSupport (Section 4, coverage polarity; Ω_W by the three facts of Definition 12 of Field Chapter 1 v2.7, segment-level, not marked by condition);
  SC_Hold (*The Necessity Form of Law* v1.4, Section 6): way two — declare the current-capacity quantity Cap_8 = Dom_8(P8) and verify on the declared family of histories that it is given by the antecedents.
Standard library and fractions only; no randomness."""
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
import jhc_core as JH
import dm21_model as G
TH = Fr(2, 3)
EPS = Fr(1, 100)
R_ = ('uP', 'cP')
NONR = ('mP', 'uQ', 'cQ', 'mQ')       # Trace-Response positions outside R (h is the sediment-supply reservoir and V is never read; neither is a candidate carrier of the relation)
I_ = ('B', 'S')
TAU_ACT, TAU_W = 3, 12

def read(host, w, i, theta=TH):
    """Read_i: whether R*_θ(P) holds on event i (determined by w and i alone)."""
    return G.rel_main(theta)(host, w, i)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Established
_TRI = {}

def carrier_triage(host, x, v):
    """The [17] three-way split of rest after supply cut-off from value v at position x (the rest map acts on each position independently and depends only on v; cached)."""
    key = (x, v)
    if key not in _TRI:
        seq, z = [], v
        for _ in range(200):
            seq.append(z)
            z = JH.floor_grid(min(host.hi[x], max(host.lo[x], z - host.p['kap'] * z)), host.grid)
        stp = lambda q: JH.floor_grid(q * (1 - host.p['kap']), host.grid)
        lb, st = JH.retention(seq, EPS, ('invariant set', stp, [Fr(0), Fr(1, 256), Fr(2, 256)]))
        _TRI[key] = JH.triage(lb, st, TAU_ACT, TAU_W)
    return _TRI[key]

def established(host, w, theta=TH):
    """Returns (value, itemized). Itemized: CC current carrying (every position of R has oriented depth > ε, and R is one organization block: both positions lie on the series path of route P);
    CR for each i: Read_i holds (the carrying extends to all covered content of I), Read_i fails once R is reset to ground state (the relation content is carried by R),
    and Read_i still holds when the Trace-Response positions outside R are reset to ground state while R is kept (R is a complete carrier); CNI there is no instantaneous drive at the end of a macro-step (Drive^inst = ∅, Freeze = identity),
    so for each i the complete carrying does not depend on instantaneous links (equivalent to CR, see 8.5, Lemma 21-13); SED the three-way split of rest after supply cut-off of every position of R gives Trace-Response carrier."""
    cc = all(JH.depth(host, w, x) > EPS for x in R_)
    cr = {}
    for i in I_:
        now = read(host, w, i, theta)
        kill = not read(host, dict(w, **{x: Fr(0) for x in R_}), i, theta)
        comp = read(host, dict(w, **{x: Fr(0) for x in NONR}), i, theta)
        cr[i] = (now, kill, comp)
    cr_ok = all(all(v) for v in cr.values())
    cni = cr_ok
    sed = {x: carrier_triage(host, x, w[x]) for x in R_}
    sed_ok = all(v == 'Trace-Response carrier' for v in sed.values())
    val = int(cc and cr_ok and cni and sed_ok)
    return val, dict(CC=cc, CR=cr, CNI=cni, SED=sed)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- DynamicRole
def admvar(host, w, ref):
    """Declared legitimate counterfactual family of R (finite): (A) keep the preset base and revert only the increments relative to the reference (every position of R takes its same-beat value in the rest reference history);
    (B) revert the increment of u_P or of c_P alone; (C) reset R to ground state 0. All values lie in the type domain and on the gridding lattice."""
    out = [('revert increments (u_P, c_P)', {x: ref[x] for x in R_}), ('revert increment (u_P)', {'uP': ref['uP']}),
           ('revert increment (c_P)', {'cP': ref['cP']}), ('reset to ground state (u_P, c_P)', {x: Fr(0) for x in R_})]
    return [(nm, v) for nm, v in out if any(w[x] != v[x] for x in v)]

def role_activity(host, w, i):
    """Actual activity and write-back of the duty on event i: (Q_P, S_P, Δu_P, Δc_P)."""
    sig, t, y = host.terms(w, i)
    return (y['QP'], y['SP'], t['P']['du'], t['P']['dc'])

def dynamic_role(host, w, ref, theta=TH):
    """Returns (value, witness list). Value = 1 if and only if there exist i ∈ I and v ∈ admvar such that the activity Q_P, S_P and the write-back Δu_P, Δc_P all change (other positions fixed).
    The witnesses list, for each (i, v), the changes and whether the read-out flips."""
    wits = []
    val = 0
    for i in I_:
        a0 = role_activity(host, w, i)
        r0 = read(host, w, i, theta)
        for nm, v in admvar(host, w, ref):
            w2 = dict(w, **v)
            a1 = role_activity(host, w2, i)
            ch = tuple(x != y for x, y in zip(a0, a1))
            flip = read(host, w2, i, theta) != r0
            wits.append((i, nm, ch, flip))
            if all(ch):
                val = 1
    return val, wits

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- ΩSupport (the three facts of Ω_W, segment-level)
def omega_support(host, mstates, mevents, t, W=8, theta=TH, nmin=2, halves=2, flows=None, conds=None):
    """Model-side reading of ΩSupport (main text 8.5). Bearing segment Q_L = R (u_P → c_P, Gully-Line γ_P of route P);
    for each i ∈ I: applicable entry e = footslope node (inflow i enters directly at the node of u_P), e ⇝*_R Q_L (structurally reachable: u_P and c_P lie in series on the same route);
    the three facts of Ω_W(Q_L, Rel(L)) (Definition 12):
      First, the re-traversal records meet the declared criterion: an event k in the window that passes along γ_P (Q_P > 0) and on which Rel(L) holds is recorded as a “bearing pass”;
          the second and later bearing passes in the window are re-traversal records (the first is only a pass, not a re-traversal); the number of records is ≥ nmin;
      Second, Brk_W(γ_P) = 0: g_P > 0 at every macro-step boundary in the window and at t (conductance is checked at macro-step boundaries), and Q_P > 0 on every event beat in the window (throughflow is checked on event beats);
      Third, the window is split as declared into halves equal segments, and every segment contains a re-traversal record.
    Ω_W belongs to the segment and is not marked by re-entry condition (*The Necessity Form of Law* v1.4 (iii-a), scope statement (1)); for the stricter condition-marked reading see dm21_model.replay_hist_path.
    flows[k], conds[k]: the actual throughflow of P on the k-th event beat and the actual conductance of P at the k-th macro-step boundary of the actual history (supplied by the caller when the actual history contains a disconnection;
    by default recomputed from the states via host). A bearing pass further requires that Rel(L) holds on that event and that the actual throughflow of that event is positive. Returns (value, itemized, records)."""
    if t < W:
        return 0, {}, {}
    fq = (lambda k: flows[k]) if flows is not None else (lambda k: host.act(mstates[k], mevents[k])['QP'])
    gc = (lambda k: conds[k]) if conds is not None else (lambda k: host.cond(mstates[k], 'P'))
    ks = list(range(t - W, t))
    passes = [k for k in ks if fq(k) > 0 and read(host, mstates[k], mevents[k], theta)]
    recs = passes[1:]
    f1 = len(recs) >= nmin
    f2 = all(gc(k) > 0 for k in ks + [t]) and all(fq(k) > 0 for k in ks)
    seg = W // halves
    f3 = all(any(t - W + j * seg <= k < t - W + (j + 1) * seg for k in recs) for j in range(halves))
    entry = all(host.cond(mstates[t], 'P') > 0 for _i in I_)      # for each i: the entry exists and is reachable via R (under fixed topology, just the existence of γ_P)
    val = int(f1 and f2 and f3 and entry)
    return val, dict(f1=f1, f2=f2, f3=f3, entry=entry), dict(passes=passes, recs=recs)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Macro-step-by-macro-step read-back along a history
def macro_trace(host, seq, w0=None, r=3):
    """Sequence of end-of-macro-step states and the same-beat state sequence of the rest reference history (reference history: the same initial state, rest beats only)."""
    w = dict(host.init if w0 is None else w0)
    ref = dict(w)
    ms, rs = [w], [ref]
    for e in seq:
        w = G.macro(host, w, e, r)
        for _ in range(r + 1):
            ref = host.step(ref, 'R')
        ms.append(w)
        rs.append(ref)
    return ms, rs

def law_readout(host, seq, w0=None, ms=None, rs=None, theta=TH, flows=None, conds=None):
    """At the end of each macro-step t: (Established, DynamicRole, ΩSupport, whether a read-out-flip witness exists, itemized)."""
    if ms is None:
        ms, rs = macro_trace(host, seq, w0)
    out = []
    for t in range(len(seq) + 1):
        e_, ed = established(host, ms[t], theta)
        d_, dw = dynamic_role(host, ms[t], rs[t], theta)
        o_, od, orec = omega_support(host, ms, list(seq), t, 8, theta, flows=flows, conds=conds)
        out.append(dict(Est=e_, DR=d_, Om=o_, flip=any(x[3] for x in dw), ed=ed, dw=dw, od=od, orec=orec))
    return out, ms, rs

def first_all(rows):
    return next((t for t, r in enumerate(rows) if r['Est'] == r['DR'] == r['Om'] == 1), None)
~~~

A.4  dm21\_run.py  Run entry 1: local model 𝔐21 (sections C0–C10)

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program: run entry of the local model 𝔐21 (Sections C0–C10).
Run: python3 dm21_run.py
Sections: C0 upstream and declarations; C1 finding the Trace-Responses (unified frame Φ8); C2 element certificates and single Trace-Responses; C3 set organization (on the element-certificate set only); C4 tendency and single-event thresholds;
    C5 activity and budget (event beats and cross-beat accounts kept separately); C6 re-action and the two kinds of write-back; C7 dominance judgment for the candidate law (Dom_N and Dom_∞ under separate protocols), order dependence and historical localization;
    C7L proxy readings of Law Group Form qualification and counterexamples (Established, DynamicRole^a/^h, Ω^d/Ω^c, Dom); C8 interventions (three kinds of switches: capacity reduction, disconnection, sediment destination);
    C9 identifiability of climate trace-leaving (exact class counts, separation margins, common subsequent drive); C10 Step 7 (TraceSet, Part, HDyn on the same T).
Each item prints “pass” or “fail”; a summary follows at the end; the exit code is 1 if any item fails. Standard library only; every numeric value is a Fraction; no randomness.
The finite verifications cover only the listed generated domain; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text."""
import hashlib, itertools, os, sys
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
~~~

sys.path\[:0\] = \[HERE\]

~~~python
import jhc_core as JH          # [17] Appendix A.1
import jxs_core as JX          # [18] Appendix A.1
import dm21_model as G
EPS = Fr(1, 100)
TH = Fr(2, 3)
R3 = 3
TAU_ACT, TAU_W = 1, 12
RES = []

def check(label, ok):
    RES.append(bool(ok))
    print(('pass ' if ok else 'fail ') + label)

fs = JH.fstr

def fl(x):
    return '%.4f' % float(x)

def H(seq, r=R3):
    out = []
    for e in seq:
        out += [e] + ['R'] * r
    return out

def macro_states(host, seq, w0=None):
    S = G.run(host, host.init if w0 is None else w0, H(seq))
    return [S[(R3 + 1) * k] for k in range(len(seq) + 1)], S

def lab_main(host, seq, theta=TH, j='P', w0=None):
    rel = G.rel_main(theta, j)
    ms, S = macro_states(host, seq, w0)
    return ''.join('*' if rel(host, ms[k], e) else ('p' if host.share(ms[k], e) >= theta else '.') for k, e in enumerate(seq)), ms

def sstr(w, keys=G.SITES):
    return '{' + ', '.join('%s=%s' % (x, fs(w[x])) for x in keys) + '}'

DIST = [('id', None), ('bar', {'mP': Fr(1, 16)}), ('bank', {'cP': -Fr(1, 16)}), ('fill', {'uP': -Fr(1, 16)})]
P8 = dict(K=2, N=8, budget=1)

def dom_string(host, seq, K=2, N=8, dists=None, budget=1, w0=None):
    ms, _ = macro_states(host, seq, w0)
    return ''.join(G.dom_N(host, w, TH, K, R3, DIST if dists is None else dists, N, budget)[0] for w in ms), ms

print('The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program run output: local model 𝔐21')
print('=' * 60)
# ============================================================ C0
print('C0  Upstream programs and declarations')
VEND = {'jhc_core.py': '792266771224a7403d8405c4d1c6e0b1dcd06d2ff057e2e14ee3567f76269edd',
        'jxs_core.py': '69614a9f07b390075b2d2dd7c340265812e6ff276e3cd6c8b2f685f28a6f3580'}
for nm, hv in VEND.items():
    got = hashlib.sha256(open(os.path.join(HERE, nm), 'rb').read()).hexdigest()
    check('C0a%d %s has the SHA-256 listed in the upstream appendix (%s…)' % (1 + list(VEND).index(nm), nm, hv[:16]), got == hv)
h0 = G.Geo()
p = h0.p
print('  Parameters: ' + ', '.join('%s=%s' % (k, fs(v) if isinstance(v, Fr) else v) for k, v in sorted(p.items())))

def stripped(host, seq):
    w, tot = dict(host.init), Fr(0)
    for e in seq:
        tot += host.supply(w, e)
        w = G.macro(host, w, e, R3)
    return tot
~~~

~~~python
# ============================================================ C3
print('\nC3  Step 3  Set organization (flow-division lemma, necessary condition for joint formation, organization hypergraph on the element-certificate set)')

def kirchhoff(host, w, Q):
    gP, gQ = host.cond(w, 'P'), host.cond(w, 'Q')
    phi = (Q + gP * w['mP'] + gQ * w['mQ']) / (gP + gQ)
    if phi - w['mP'] <= 0:
        return Fr(0)
    if phi - w['mQ'] <= 0:
        return Q
    return gP * (phi - w['mP'])
lattice = []
vals_u = [Fr(-1, 2), Fr(-1, 4), Fr(0), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)]
vals_m = [Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1)]
for uP, cP, uQ, cQ in itertools.product(vals_u, repeat=4):
    for mP, mQ in itertools.product(vals_m, repeat=2):
        lattice.append({'uP': uP, 'cP': cP, 'uQ': uQ, 'cQ': cQ, 'mP': mP, 'mQ': mQ, 'h': Fr(1), 'V': Fr(0)})
check('C3a the closed form of the flow division equals the direct solution of the node equation at every one of %d lattice points × 2 inflows (Lemma 21-1 (1))' % len(lattice),
      all(h0.route(w, Q)[0] == kirchhoff(h0, w, Q) for w in lattice for Q in (Fr(9, 4), Fr(4))))
bad = 0
for w in lattice:
    q0 = h0.route(w, Fr(4))[0]
    for x, sgn in (('uP', 1), ('cP', 1), ('mQ', 1), ('uQ', -1), ('cQ', -1), ('mP', -1)):
        q1 = h0.route(dict(w, **{x: w[x] + Fr(1, 4)}), Fr(4))[0]
        bad += sgn * (q1 - q0) < 0
check('C3b water seeks the low ground (Lemma 21-1 (2)): Q_P is nondecreasing in u_P, c_P, m_Q and nonincreasing in u_Q, c_Q, m_P (no counterexample on the lattice)', bad == 0)
S1all = G.run(h0, h0.init, H(SEQ1))
S2all = G.run(h0, h0.init, H(SEQ2))
n = 0
okn = True
for w in lattice + S1all + S2all:
    for e in ('B', 'S'):
        Q = h0.ev[e][0]
        gP, gQ = h0.cond(w, 'P'), h0.cond(w, 'Q')
        if h0.route(w, Q)[0] >= TH * Q:
            den = (1 - TH) * Q + gQ * (w['mQ'] - w['mP'])
            need = TH * Q * gQ / den if den > 0 else None
            n += 1
            okn &= den > 0 and gP >= need and min(h0.seg_cond(w['uP']), h0.seg_cond(w['cP'])) >= need
check('C3c necessary condition for joint formation (Theorem 21-2): whenever Q_P ≥ θQ, the mouth-bar difference bound holds, and g_P and the smaller of the two segment conductances are both at least θQg_Q/((1 − θ)Q + g_QΔm) (%d cases)' % n, okn)
~~~

~~~python
sinkV = all(('V' not in nd) or (k == 'history condition' and tg.endswith('@V')) for (k, tg, nd) in hyperV)
check('C3f process graph (informal hypergraph): with V added, V appears only as a write target and never in a co-participation hyperedge or in the setting subset of a write to another position (sink)', sinkV)
# ============================================================ C4
print('\nC4  Step 4  Historical dynamics forms tendency (tendency Φ_σ, loop direction, single-event competition threshold and compatibility gate)')
JHo = G.jxs_host(h0)
ms1, _ = macro_states(h0, SEQ1)
ms2, _ = macro_states(h0, SEQ2)
for nm, w in (('initial terrain', h0.init), ('end of H1 macro-step 8 (w8)', w8), ('end of H2 macro-step 8', ms2[8])):

    print('  Tendency Φ_σ (%s): ' % nm + '; '.join('%s → Q_P/Q %s, S_P %s, S_Q %s' % (e, fl(pot[e]['QP'] / h0.ev[e][0]), fl(pot[e]['SP']), fl(pot[e]['SQ'])) for e in 'BS'))

check('C4a the tendency precedes activity on the signal class: w8 gives different activity for B and S, and for both gives P the main throughflow', potg['B'] != potg['S'] and all(potg[e]['QP'] >= TH * h0.ev[e][0] for e in 'BS'))

def loop_sign(host, w, x, e):
    Q, sm, lev = host.ev[e]
    sig = host.supply(w, e)
    qs = [host.act(dict(w, **{x: w[x] + Fr(k, 32)}), e)['QP'] for k in range(-2, 3)]
    mpsi = 1 if all(qs[i] <= qs[i + 1] for i in range(4)) else (-1 if all(qs[i] >= qs[i + 1] for i in range(4)) else 0)
    key = {'uP': 'du', 'cP': 'dc', 'mP': 'mouth'}[x]
    ws = [G.pterms(host.p, qs[2] + Fr(k, 32), Q, sig, w['mP'], lev)[key] for k in range(-2, 3)]
    mw = 1 if all(ws[i] <= ws[i + 1] for i in range(4)) else (-1 if all(ws[i] >= ws[i + 1] for i in range(4)) else 0)
    return mpsi, mw
signs = {x: loop_sign(h0, w8, x, 'B') for x in ('uP', 'cP', 'mP')}
print('  Loop direction (w8, inflow B): ' + '; '.join('%s: ψ %+d, write %+d, loop %+d' % (x, a, b, a * b) for x, (a, b) in signs.items()))
check('C4b direction law: gully mouth and channel reach form positive loops, the mouth bar a negative loop', signs['uP'][0] * signs['uP'][1] == 1 and signs['cP'][0] * signs['cP'][1] == 1 and signs['mP'][0] * signs['mP'][1] == -1)

def mstar0(p, QP, load1):
    """Channel-reach backfilling threshold without the lake level, m*₀ = Lb[S0 − (ωt + load₁/kt)/Q_P]; Theorem 21-5 (B): when load₁ > 0, Δc < 0 ⇔ m_P + ℓ > m*₀."""
    return p['Lb'] * (p['S0'] - (p['wt'] + load1 / p['kt']) / QP)

def qstar_e(p, Q, sig):
    return p['wt'] / (p['Se'] - sig / (Q * p['kt']))
bad = n = 0
for e in ('B', 'S'):
    Q, sm, _lev = h0.ev[e]
    for lev in (Fr(0), Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)):
        for qi in range(1, 65):
            QP = Q * qi / 64
            for sig in (Fr(1, 16), sm):
                t0 = G.pterms(p, QP, Q, sig, Fr(0), lev)
                n += 1
                bad += ((t0['du'] < 0) != (QP < qstar_e(p, Q, sig)))
                if t0['load1'] > 0:
                    m0 = mstar0(p, QP, t0['load1'])
                    for mi in range(0, 33):
                        m = Fr(mi, 16)
                        t = G.pterms(p, QP, Q, sig, m, lev)
                        n += 1
                        bad += ((t['dc'] < 0) != (m + lev > m0))

QS, smS, _ = h0.ev['S']
print('  Low flow Q*_e = %s (share %s); high flow Q*_e = %s (share %s)' % (fs(qstar_e(p, QS, smS)), fs(qstar_e(p, QS, smS) / QS), fs(qstar_e(p, Fr(4), Fr(1, 4))), fl(qstar_e(p, Fr(4), Fr(1, 4)) / 4)))
gate = qstar_e(p, QS, smS) / QS == TH
viol = 0
for w in lattice + S1all + S2all:
    if G.rel_main(TH)(h0, w, 'S'):
        sig, tt, y = h0.terms(w, 'S')
        viol += tt['P']['du'] < 0
check('C4d compatibility gate (Theorem 21-10 (2′)): under the declared parameters Q*_e(S) = θQ_S, so whenever R* holds at a low-flow read, Δu_P ≥ 0; conversely, if a low flow buries the P mouth (0 < Q_P < Q*_e), R* fails at that same read (the next beat is not involved; no counterexample on the lattice or the two histories)',
      gate and viol == 0)
# counterexample F21-1: the initial mouth bar exceeds the backfilling threshold (Corollary 21-6a must include the initial value)
hm = G.Geo(par={'mu': Fr(3, 4)})
rhoM = (1 - hm.p['mu']) ** R3
outmaxM = max(sm + hm.p['ke'] * G.pos(Q * hm.p['Se'] - hm.p['wc']) + hm.p['ke'] * G.pos(Q * hm.p['S0'] - hm.p['wc']) for (Q, sm, _l) in hm.ev.values())
mbarM = hm.p['beta'] * outmaxM * rhoM / (1 - rhoM)
wx = dict(hm.init, uP=Fr(3), cP=Fr(3), mP=Fr(2))
sig, tt, y = hm.terms(wx, 'B')
m0x = mstar0(hm.p, y['QP'], tt['P']['load1'])
print('  Counterexample F21-1: for μ = 3/4, m̄ = %s; initial state m_P = 2, Q_P = %s, load₁ = %s, m*₀ = %s, Δc = %s' % (fs(mbarM), fs(y['QP']), fs(tt['P']['load1']), fs(m0x), fs(tt['P']['dc'])))
check('C4e Corollary 21-6a must use the macro-step bound max(m_P(0), m̄) + bΔ: in counterexample F21-1, m̄ = 1/56 < m*₀ while the initial mouth bar 2 > m*₀, and the channel reach backfills (Δc < 0), consistent with the corollary',
      mbarM == Fr(1, 56) and m0x == Fr(17, 16) and tt['P']['dc'] < 0 and max(wx['mP'], mbarM) > m0x)
# ============================================================ C5
print('\nC5  Step 5  Tendency carried out as activity (flow division and sediment delivery to the lake for each inflow; budget kept in separate accounts)')
for nm, ms, seq in (('H1', ms1, SEQ1), ('H2', ms2, SEQ2)):
    print('  %s: ' % nm + ' '.join('%s%s/%s/%s' % (e, fl(h0.act(ms[k], e)['QP'] / h0.ev[e][0])[1:4], fl(h0.act(ms[k], e)['SP'])[:4], fl(h0.act(ms[k], e)['SQ'])[:4]) for k, e in enumerate(seq)))
conn2 = [h0.act(ms2[k], e)['SP'] + h0.act(ms2[k], e)['SQ'] for k, e in enumerate(SEQ2) if e == 'S']
conn1 = [h0.act(ms1[k], e)['SP'] for k, e in enumerate(SEQ1) if e == 'S']
check('C5a intermittent connectivity: in the early part of H2 every low flow delivers 0 sediment to the lake; in the late part of H1 every low flow delivers sediment > 0 to the lake via P', all(c == 0 for c in conn2) and all(c > 0 for c in conn1))

def ledger(host, hist):
    """Separate accounts: the pre-booking budget at event beats; the totals booked over all beats, by process class; the post-processing residual split into a clipping part and a gridding part."""
    S = G.run(host, host.init, hist)
    worst = Fr(0)
    cat = {}
    clamp = {x: Fr(0) for x in G.SITES}
    grid = {x: Fr(0) for x in G.SITES}
    for k, s in enumerate(hist):
        w = S[k]
        y = host.act(w, s)
        raw = {x: Fr(0) for x in G.SITES}
        for (pp, x, c) in host.comps(w, y, s):
            raw[x] += c
            cat[(pp, x)] = cat.get((pp, x), Fr(0)) + c
        if s in host.ev:
            bal = -raw['h'] + sum(raw['u' + j] + raw['c' + j] for j in 'PQ') - sum(raw['m' + j] for j in 'PQ') - raw['V']
            worst = max(worst, abs(bal))
        for x in G.SITES:
            v = w[x] + raw[x]
            vc = min(host.hi[x], max(host.lo[x], v))
            vf = JH.floor_grid(vc, host.grid)
            clamp[x] += vc - v
            grid[x] += vf - vc
            assert vf == S[k + 1][x]
    ident = all(S[-1][x] - S[0][x] == sum(c for (pp, y), c in cat.items() if y == x) + clamp[x] + grid[x] for x in G.SITES)
    return worst, cat, clamp, grid, ident
wb1, cat1, cl1, gr1, id1 = ledger(h0, H(SEQ1))
wb2, cat2, cl2, gr2, id2 = ledger(h0, H(SEQ2))
check('C5b the pre-booking budget at event beats closes exactly: hillslope stripping + net scour on both routes = mouth-bar increment + lake-basin increment (residual 0 at every event beat of H1 and H2)', wb1 == 0 and wb2 == 0)
for nm, cat, cl, gr in (('H1', cat1, cl1, gr1), ('H2', cat2, cl2, gr2)):
    tot = lambda pp: sum(c for (q, x), c in cat.items() if q == pp)
    print('  %s whole-run accounts: hillslope stripping %s; weathering supply %s; freeze-thaw supply %s; net gully-mouth scour %s; net channel-reach scour %s; sediment retained on mouth bars %s; sediment into the lake basin %s; removed from mouth bars by lake-wave reworking %s; diffusive infilling (u and c combined) %s'
          % (nm, fl(-tot('strip')), fl(tot('weather')), fl(tot('frost')), fl(tot('scour_e')), fl(tot('scour_c')), fl(tot('mouth')), fl(tot('basin')), fl(-tot('rework')), fl(-tot('diff'))))
    print('     Clipping residual (overflow at the burial limit, etc.): ' + ', '.join('%s %s' % (x, fl(cl[x])) for x in G.SITES if cl[x] != 0) + '; gridding residual: ' + ', '.join('%s %s' % (x, fl(gr[x])) for x in G.SITES if gr[x] != 0))
~~~

~~~python
# ============================================================ C7L
print('\nC7L  Proxy readings of Law Group Form qualification and counterexamples (R = {u_P, c_P}; I = {B, S}; W = 8 macro-steps; main text 8.6; for the formal read-back see dm21_run_law.py)')
R_ = ['uP', 'cP']

def est_at(host, w):
    """Model-side reading of Established: CurrentCarrier (every position of R deeper than ε) ∧ CarrierRealizes (R* currently holds for both inflow classes in I, and resetting R to the ground state makes it fail for some class)
    ∧ CarrierNonInstant (the three-way split of every position of R under supply cut-off rest from w gives a Trace-Response carrier)."""
    if not all(w[x] > EPS for x in R_):
        return 0
    if not all(G.rel_main(TH)(host, w, e) for e in 'BS'):
        return 0
    if all(G.rel_main(TH)(host, dict(w, uP=Fr(0), cP=Fr(0)), e) for e in 'BS'):
        return 0
    for x in R_:
        ww, seq = dict(w), []
        for t in range(200):
            seq.append(ww[x])
            ww = host.step(ww, 'R')
        stp = lambda v: JH.floor_grid(v * (1 - host.p['kap']), host.grid)
        lb, st = JH.retention(seq, EPS, ('invariant set', stp, [Fr(0), Fr(1, 256), Fr(2, 256)]))
        if JH.triage(lb, st, TAU_ACT, TAU_W) != 'Trace-Response carrier':
            return 0
    return 1

def dyn_at(host, beats, t, W=8):
    """Model-side reading of DynamicRole: on the beats of the window [t − W, t), [17] Definition 6′ on T = R gives effective difference certified."""
    if t < 1:
        return 0
    a = (R3 + 1) * max(0, t - W)
    b = (R3 + 1) * t
    st, _w = JH.hdyn_cert(host, beats[0][a:b + 1], beats[1][a:b], R_, EPS)
    return int(st == '1')

def dyn_formed(host, states, refs, sigs, t, W=8):
    """DynamicRole^h, formed-position reading (excludes witnesses from a preset initial state): at beat k of the window [t − W, t), the set positions are taken only from the “formed positions”—
    oriented depth > ε, and difference along the orientation from the rest reference history (same initial state, rest beats only) > ε (the difference is written by the recorded history and is always 0 at the start, so preset old grooves do not enter);
    on these positions the effective difference is judged beat by beat and process by process with hc_status of [17] Definition 6′. Returns (value, witness (beat, signal, process, position, formed positions, set positions))."""
    if t < 1:
        return 0, None
    a, b = (R3 + 1) * max(0, t - W), (R3 + 1) * t
    for k in range(a, b):
        w, s_ = states[k], sigs[k]
        U = [x for x in R_ if JH.depth(host, w, x) > EPS and host.orient[x] * (w[x] - refs[k][x]) > EPS]
        if not U:
            continue
        y = host.act(w, s_)
        seen = set()
        for (pr, x, _c) in host.comps(w, y, s_):
            if x not in R_ or (pr, x) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((pr, x))
            if abs(JH.comp_of(host, w, y, s_, pr, x)) <= EPS:
                continue
            st, wt = JH.hc_status(host, w, s_, pr, x, U, EPS)
            if st == 'effective difference certified':
                return 1, (k, s_, pr, x, U, wt[0])
    return 0, None
SEQ1m = 'B' * 8 + 'SB' * 4
qual = {}
for nm, seq in (('H1', SEQ1), ('H1m', SEQ1m), ('H2', SEQ2)):
    ms_, S_ = macro_states(h0, seq)
    hist = H(seq)
    REF_ = G.run(h0, h0.init, ['R'] * len(hist))
    rows = []
    for t in range(len(seq) + 1):
        e_ = est_at(h0, ms_[t])
        d_ = dyn_at(h0, (S_, hist), t)
        o_, _x = G.replay_hist(h0, ms_, list(seq), t, 8, TH)
        dm = G.dom_N(h0, ms_[t], TH, 2, R3, DIST, 8, 1)[0]
        df_, dwit = dyn_formed(h0, S_, REF_, hist, t)
        op_, _f, orec = G.replay_hist_path(h0, ms_, list(seq), t, 8, TH)
        rows.append((e_, d_, o_, dm, df_, op_, dwit, orec))
    qual[nm] = rows
    print('  %s: Established %s; DynamicRole^a (all-position reading) %s; Ω^d (gully-depth proxy) %s; Dom_8(P8) %s' % (nm, ''.join(str(r[0]) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r[1]) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r[2]) for r in rows), ''.join(r[3] for r in rows)))
    print('  %s: DynamicRole^h (formed-position reading) %s; Ω^c (class-labeled path reading) %s' % (nm, ''.join(str(r[4]) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r[5]) for r in rows)))
eh1, eh1m, eh2 = qual['H1'], qual['H1m'], qual['H2']
~~~

~~~text
      % (wH2[0], wH2[6], d2[0] == REF2[0], [fs(d2[k]['uP'] - REF2[k]['uP']) for k in (1, 2, 3)]))
~~~

~~~python
def sed_run(par, remove_each=False):
    """Returns (string for the challenge after treatment stops, cumulative accounts, final state, continued-treatment challenge string, lake-entry target string). Challenge after treatment stops: the interventions of the history run up to the read point, and the challenge uses ordinary macro-steps;
    continued-treatment challenge: every macro-step of the challenge follows the frozen order “event → rest → bar clearing m_P := 0 → perturbation” (periodic-removal arm only);
    lake-entry target: the relation rel_basin(θ) whose target quantity is the actual sediment into the lake basin, terms['basin'] (the periodic-removal arm uses continued-treatment macro-steps)."""
    hv = G.Geo(par=par)
    w = dict(hv.init)
    acc = dict(outP=Fr(0), barP=Fr(0), basinP=Fr(0), export=Fr(0), removed=Fr(0))
    ds, dc, db = '', '', ''
    mac = G.macro_cleared if remove_each else None
    for e in SEQ1 + '.':
        ds += G.dom_N(hv, w, TH, 2, R3, DIST, 8, 1)[0]
        dc += G.dom_N(hv, w, TH, 2, R3, DIST, 8, 1, mac=G.macro_cleared)[0] if remove_each else '-'
        db += G.dom_N(hv, w, TH, 2, R3, DIST, 8, 1, rel=G.rel_basin(TH), mac=mac)[0]
        if e == '.':
            break
        sig, tt, y = hv.terms(w, e)
        acc['outP'] += tt['P']['out']
        acc['barP'] += tt['P']['mouth']
        acc['basinP'] += tt['P']['basin']
        acc['export'] += tt['P']['export']
        w = G.macro(hv, w, e, R3)
        if remove_each:
            acc['removed'] += w['mP']
            w = dict(w, mP=Fr(0))
    return ds, acc, w, dc, db
SEDV = [('no intervention (moderate backwater Lb = 1)', {'Lb': Fr(1)}, False), ('periodic mouth-bar removal (m_P := 0 at every macro-step end, removed material exported)', {'Lb': Fr(1)}, True),
        ('sustained mouth-bar deposition ban, all channel-exit sediment to the lake basin (β_P = 0)', {'Lb': Fr(1), 'betaP': Fr(0)}, False),
        ('sustained mouth-bar deposition ban, former bar share exported (β_P = 0, export)', {'Lb': Fr(1), 'betaP': Fr(0), 'exportP': True}, False)]
sedres = {}
for nm, par, rem in SEDV:
    ds, acc, wend, dc, db = sed_run(par, rem)
    sedres[nm] = (ds, acc, wend, dc, db)
    print('  %s: Dom_8 (challenge after treatment stops) %s; Dom_8 (continued-treatment challenge) %s; Dom_8 (lake-entry target) %s'
          % (nm, ds, dc, db))
    print('    Material accounts (rerouting and export reported separately): channel-exit sediment on route P %s; retained on the mouth bar %s; into the lake basin %s; exported %s; periodically removed %s; final m_P %s'
          % (fl(acc['outP']), fl(acc['barP']), fl(acc['basinP']), fl(acc['export']), fl(acc['removed']), fs(wend['mP'])))
a0, a1, a2, a3 = [sedres[nm] for nm, _p, _r in SEDV]
wq = G.Geo(par={'Lb': Fr(1)})
w3 = dict(wq.init)
for _ in range(3):
    w3 = dict(G.macro(wq, w3, 'B', R3), mP=Fr(0))
print('  Next high flow after the end of macro-step 3 of the periodic-removal history: ordinary macro-step successor m_P = %s; continued-treatment macro-step successor m_P = %s' % (fs(G.macro(wq, w3, 'B', R3)['mP']), fs(G.macro_cleared(wq, w3, 'B', R3)['mP'])))
check('C8e periodic removal split into two protocols (counterexample F21-6: the ordinary successor does not continue the treatment): “formative history under periodic removal, then the P8 challenge after treatment stops” and “continued-treatment challenge” (frozen order: event → rest → bar clearing → perturbation) are output separately; the two strings are identical and both first reach 1 at the end of macro-step 3, while the successors of the two protocols differ (ordinary successor m_P > 0, continued-treatment successor m_P = 0)',
      a1[3] == a1[0] and a1[3].index('1') == 3 and G.macro(wq, w3, 'B', R3)['mP'] > 0 and G.macro_cleared(wq, w3, 'B', R3)['mP'] == 0)
check('C8f lake-entry target: with the actual sediment into the lake basin, terms[basin], as the target quantity, candidate dominance is still not generated without intervention, and the three interventions still generate it at the end of macro-step 3 (in this example the sediment into the lake basin on route Q is 0, and the lake-entry share and the channel-exit share both equal 1, so the two target strings coincide; this coincidence is limited to this example)',
      '1' not in a0[4] and a1[4] == a2[4] == a3[4] == a1[0])
check('C8d the three sediment-destination switches report absolute amounts separately; the equivalence statement is limited to the declared throughflow and channel-exit readings: without intervention candidate dominance is not generated; all three interventions generate it at the end of macro-step 3; the three have the same channel-exit sediment, while the absolute amounts retained on the bar, delivered to the lake, exported and removed all differ',
      '1' not in a0[0] and a1[0] == a2[0] == a3[0] and a1[0].index('1') == 3 and a1[1]['outP'] == a2[1]['outP'] == a3[1]['outP']
      and a2[1]['barP'] == 0 and a3[1]['export'] > 0 and a1[1]['removed'] > 0 and a2[1]['basinP'] != a3[1]['basinP'])
# ============================================================ C9
print('\nC9  How the climate law stays in the landform (same initial terrain, same cumulative precipitation and freeze-thaw amount, different seasonal orders; exact class counts and separation margins reported separately)')
BLK = {'F': ['F'] * 4, 'B': ['B', 'R', 'R', 'R'], 'S': ['S', 'R', 'R', 'R']}
orders = sorted(set(itertools.permutations('FBSS')))
YEARS = 4

def climate_run(host, order):
    hist = []
    for _ in range(YEARS):
        for b in order:
            hist += BLK[b]
    S = G.run(host, host.init, hist)
    layers = [S[k + 1]['V'] - S[k]['V'] for k, s in enumerate(hist) if s in host.ev]
    return S, layers, hist

def readouts(wend, layers):
    return {
        'terrain (both gully mouths, channel reaches, mouth bars)': tuple(wend[x] for x in ('uP', 'cP', 'mP', 'uQ', 'cQ', 'mQ')),
        'hillslope regolith h': (wend['h'],),
        'lake-basin cumulative V': (wend['V'],),
        'lake-basin annual layers (event layers summed within known year boundaries)': tuple(sum(layers[i:i + 3]) for i in range(0, len(layers), 3)),
        'lake-basin event layers': tuple(layers),
        'terrain + hillslope + lake-basin cumulative': tuple(wend[x] for x in G.DYN) + (wend['V'],),
    }
regimes = [('ample sediment supply (h₀ = 3)', dict(h0.init, h=Fr(3))), ('limited sediment supply (h₀ = 0)', dict(h0.init, h=Fr(0)))]
clim = {}
for rn, init in regimes:
    hc_ = G.Geo(init=init)
    runs = {o: climate_run(hc_, o) for o in orders}
    ro = {o: readouts(runs[o][0][-1], runs[o][1]) for o in orders}
    cnt, marg = {}, {}
    for key in ro[orders[0]]:
        cls = {}
        for o in orders:
            cls.setdefault(ro[o][key], []).append(''.join(o))
        cnt[key] = sorted(cls.values())
        ms_ = [max(abs(a - b) for a, b in zip(ro[o1][key], ro[o2][key])) for o1, o2 in itertools.combinations(orders, 2) if ro[o1][key] != ro[o2][key]]
        marg[key] = min(ms_) if ms_ else None
    minh = min(S[k]['h'] for o in orders for (S, _l, hist) in [runs[o]] for k, s in enumerate(hist) if s in hc_.ev)
    maxh = max(w['h'] for o in orders for w in runs[o][0])
    clim[rn] = (hc_, runs, cnt, marg, minh, maxh)
    print('  %s: exact class counts and separation margins (minimum over pairs of distinct classes of the largest componentwise reading difference) for 12 seasonal orders (each year 4 freeze-thaw beats, 1 high flow, 2 low flows; %d years):' % (rn, YEARS))
    for key in cnt:
        print('    %s: %d classes; separation margin %s' % (key, len(cnt[key]), fs(marg[key]) if marg[key] is not None else '—'))
hcA, runsA, cntA, margA, minhA, maxhA = clim['ample sediment supply (h₀ = 3)']
hcB, runsB, cntB, margB, minhB, maxhB = clim['limited sediment supply (h₀ = 0)']
pat = lambda o: ''.join(c for c in o if c != 'F')
sameclass = all(runsA[o1][0][-1] == runsA[o2][0][-1] and runsA[o1][1] == runsA[o2][1] for o1 in orders for o2 in orders if pat(o1) == pat(o2))
~~~

A.5  dm21\_run\_lake.py  Run entry 2: river–lake feedback and avulsion succession (sections X0–X5)

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program: run entry for river–lake feedback and avulsion succession (extended model 𝔐21⁺, dm21_lake.py; sections X0–X5).
Separate entry from dm21_run.py (local baseline 𝔐21); standard library and fractions only; no randomness.
~~~

~~~python
import dm21_model as G0
import dm21_lake as X
RES = []
TH = Fr(2, 3)

def check(label, ok):
    RES.append(bool(ok))
    print('  [%s] %s' % ('pass' if ok else 'fail', label))

def fs(x):
    return str(x)

def fl(x):
    return '%.4f' % float(x)

def H(blocks):
    out = []
    for b in blocks:
        out += ['F'] * 4 if b == 'F' else [b, 'R', 'R', 'R']
    return out

CODE = {'original path kept': 'O', 'new path bears same duty': 'N', 'same-kind relation only': 'k', 'duty interrupted': 'x'}
YEARS = 15
FORCING = 'FBSS' * YEARS     # fixed inflow: each year 4 freeze-thaw beats, 1 high flow, 2 low flows (each followed by 3 rest beats); identical in both arms
print('The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program run output: river–lake feedback and avulsion succession (𝔐21⁺)')
print('Parameters (added ones): ' + ', '.join('%s=%s' % (k, fs(X.XBASE[k])) for k in ('AL', 'kV', 'ko', 'z0', 'SD', 'keD', 'wcD', 'ev', 'Hs', 'd0', 'lenN', 'S0N', 'WLmax', 'cDmax')))
# ============================================================ X0
print('\nX0  Embedding of the local baseline: working domain = event lake level 0 (the declared event class of 𝔐21), lake level clamped at ℓ₀ = 0, crevassing disabled, same parameter and switch semantics (closed, betaP, exportP); proof in main text 11.3 (Proposition 21-11); below, verification within this domain')
g0 = G0.Geo()
hx0 = X.GeoX(par={'clamp': True, 'avulse': False})
hist0 = H('BSBBSFBSSB')
A = G0.run(g0, g0.init, hist0)
B = X.run(hx0, hx0.init, hist0)
~~~

~~~text
for par in ({}, {'betaP': Fr(0)}, {'betaP': Fr(0), 'exportP': True}, {'closed': ('P',)}, {'Lb': Fr(1)}):
    ga = G0.Geo(par=par)
    gb = X.GeoX(par=dict(par, clamp=True, avulse=False))
    for k in range(16):
        hh = H(''.join('BS'[(k >> i) & 1] for i in range(4)) + 'F' + ''.join('SB'[(k >> i) & 1] for i in range(4)))
        Sa, Sb = G0.run(ga, ga.init, hh), X.run(gb, gb.init, hh)
        emb_ok = emb_ok and all(all(a[x] == b[x] for x in G0.SITES) for a, b in zip(Sa, Sb))
        emb_n += 1
check('X0b finite verification within the working domain: 5 parameter and switch semantics (default, betaP = 0, betaP = 0 with export, route P disconnected, Lb = 1) × 16 event sequences (36 beats each), %d histories in all; the two models agree beat by beat on all positions of 𝔐21' % emb_n, emb_ok)
# start: formative history of 8 high flows in the clamp arm (as w8 of 𝔐21); then lake water volume and lake-basin deposit are reset to zero, giving the common start of both arms
w0 = dict(hx0.init)
for e in H('B' * 8):
    w0 = hx0.step(w0, e)
w0 = dict(w0, WL=Fr(0), V=Fr(0))
print('  Common start w₀: ' + ', '.join('%s=%s' % (x, fl(w0[x])) for x in ('uP', 'cP', 'mP', 'uQ', 'cQ', 'mQ', 'xP', 'xQ', 'h')))
# ============================================================ X1
print('\nX1  The lake-basin state enters the reads and writes of at least two water routes (each state changes a single lake-basin item; initial terrain ι and a high flow, chosen so that both routes are above threshold)')
hf = X.GeoX()
wi = dict(hf.init)
wl = dict(wi, WL=Fr(8))
wv = dict(wi, V=Fr(32))
rows = {}
for nm, w in (('ι', wi), ('lake water volume WL := 8', wl), ('lake-basin deposit V := 32', wv)):
    _s, t, qs = hf.terms(w, 'B')
    rows[nm] = (hf.level(w), t['P']['Sc'], t['P']['dc'], t['Q']['Sc'], t['Q']['dc'], hf.outflow_pot(w), hf.outflow(w, 'B'), hf.outflow(w, 'R'))
    print('  %s: lake level ℓ %s; route P reach backwater slope %s, scour and deposition %s; route Q reach backwater slope %s, scour and deposition %s; potential lake-outlet outflow %s; realized outflow (high-flow beat) %s, (rest beat) %s' % ((nm,) + tuple(fl(v) for v in rows[nm])))
KS = ('lake water volume WL := 8', 'lake-basin deposit V := 32')
~~~

check('X1a lake water volume and lake-basin deposit both raise the lake level (ℓ = WL/AL + kV·V); the lake level changes, on both water routes P and Q, the backwater slope of the channel reach (read) and the scour-and-deposition write (write), and makes the potential lake-outlet outflow positive and the realized outflow on a high-flow beat positive',  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;all(rows\[k\]\[0\] \> rows\['ι'\]\[0\] for k in KS)  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;and all(rows\[k\]\[i\] != rows\['ι'\]\[i\] for k in KS for i in (1, 2, 3, 4)) and all(rows\[k\]\[5\] \> 0 and rows\[k\]\[6\] \> 0 for k in KS) and rows\['ι'\]\[5\] == 0)  
wdry = hf.step(wv, 'R')  
check('X1c counterexample F21-5 (outlet boundary): on a rest beat with WL = 0, V = 32 the potential outflow is %s while the available water is 0, so the realized outflow is 0 and the final water volume is %s; outlet incision is written from the realized outflow (c\_D unchanged: %s)'  
&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;&nbsp;% (fs(hf.outflow\_pot(wv)), fs(wdry\['WL'\]), fs(wdry\['cD'\])), hf.outflow(wv, 'R') == 0 and wdry\['WL'\] == 0 and wdry\['cD'\] == wv\['cD'\])  
hc = X.GeoX(par={'clamp': True})  
check('X1b under the same changes the clamp arm leaves the lake level, the scour and deposition of both routes, and the outflow unchanged (clamping cuts only the read-out of the lake-basin state; bookkeeping goes on as usual)',

~~~python
      all(hc.terms(w, 'B')[1][j]['dc'] == hc.terms(wi, 'B')[1][j]['dc'] for j in 'PQ' for w in (wl, wv)) and all(hc.outflow_pot(w) == hc.outflow_pot(wi) for w in (wl, wv)))

# ============================================================ X2
def traj(par, forcing=FORCING, w=None, periodic=False, metric='out'):
    """Run one trajectory under the fixed inflow and register it. periodic: after each event block (event beat + 3 rest beats) clear the route P mouth bar (m_P := 0; the removed material is exported and counted in removed);
    metric: sediment flux used by the duty criterion ('out' leaving the channel, 'basin' actually entering the lake). Also records the sediment budget (route P channel exit, total into the lake basin, export, removal) and the event at which N first actually takes a majority of the flow."""
    h = X.GeoX(par=par)
    w = dict(w0 if w is None else w)
    hold0 = X.role_holder(h, w, TH, metric)
    reg = dict(s='', who=[], kind=[], avul=None, mother=None, beat=None, states=[], maj=None, majev=None,
               acc=dict(QQ=Fr(0), dcQ=Fr(0), QD=Fr(0), QP=Fr(0), QN=Fr(0), outP=Fr(0), basin=Fr(0), export=Fr(0), removed=Fr(0)))
    beat = 0
    for b in forcing:
        if b != 'F':
            if reg['maj'] is None and h.exists(w, 'N') and X.majority(h, w, b):
                reg['maj'], reg['majev'] = len(reg['s']) + 1, b
            hold = X.role_holder(h, w, TH, metric)
            kinds = X.same_kind(h, w)
            c = X.classify(hold0, hold, kinds)
            reg['s'] += CODE[c]
            reg['who'].append(''.join(hold))
            reg['kind'].append(''.join(kinds))
            reg['states'].append(w)
            m = h.avulsion_due(w, b)
            if m is not None and reg['avul'] is None:
                reg['avul'], reg['mother'], reg['beat'] = len(reg['s']) - 1, m, beat
        for e in H([b]):
            y = h.act(w, e)
            if e in h.ev:
                _s, t, _q = h.terms(w, e)
                reg['acc']['QQ'] += y['QQ']
                reg['acc']['dcQ'] += t['Q']['dc']
                reg['acc']['QP'] += y['QP']
                reg['acc']['QN'] += y['QN']
                reg['acc']['outP'] += t['P']['out']
                reg['acc']['basin'] += sum(t[j]['basin'] for j in t)
                reg['acc']['export'] += sum(t[j]['export'] for j in t)
            reg['acc']['QD'] += y['QD']
            w = h.step(w, e)
            beat += 1
        if periodic and b != 'F':
            reg['acc']['removed'] += w['mP']
            w = dict(w, mP=Fr(0))
    reg['end'] = w
    reg['hold0'] = hold0
    reg['host'] = h
    return reg

print('\nX2  Fixed inflow (%d years; each year freeze-thaw · high flow · low flow · low flow): lake-basin feedback-on arm vs clamp arm (ℓ ≡ 0); readings on the other water route Q and on the lake-outlet channel D' % YEARS)
ON = traj({})
CL = traj({'clamp': True})
for nm, r in (('feedback on', ON), ('clamped', CL)):
    e = r['end']
    print('  %s: initial duty bearer %s; per-event register %s' % (nm, r['hold0'], r['s']))
    print('    Route Q cumulative event throughflow %s, cumulative event scour and deposition %s; route P cumulative throughflow %s; route N cumulative throughflow %s; cumulative lake-outlet outflow %s; final c_Q %s, c_D %s, lake level %s'
          % (fl(r['acc']['QQ']), fl(r['acc']['dcQ']), fl(r['acc']['QP']), fl(r['acc']['QN']), fl(r['acc']['QD']), fl(e['cQ']), fl(e['cD']), fl(r['host'].level(e))))
check('X2a same inflow in both arms, different readings on the other water route: the cumulative event throughflow of route Q is larger in the feedback-on arm than in the clamp arm (the scour and deposition of the Q reach is 0 in both arms, below the scour threshold, reported separately as it stands); the lake-outlet channel D carries outflow and incises only in the feedback-on arm (in the clamp arm ℓ₀ lies below the spillway sill)',
      ON['acc']['QQ'] > CL['acc']['QQ'] and ON['acc']['dcQ'] == CL['acc']['dcQ'] == 0 and ON['acc']['QD'] > 0 and ON['end']['cD'] > 0
      and CL['acc']['QD'] == 0 and CL['end']['cD'] == 0)
check('X2b clamp arm: no crevassing over the whole run, the original path P keeps the duty (every event is “original path kept”)', CL['avul'] is None and set(CL['s']) == {'O'})
# ============================================================ X3
print('\nX3  Registering formation, avulsion, succession and failure along the actual trajectory (feedback-on arm; duty and criterion frozen in advance, see main text 11.4)')
k_av = ON['avul']
wa = ON['states'][k_av]
print('  Avulsion: event %d (beat %d of the whole history) is a high flow, parent route %s; before the beat x_%s − c_%s = %s ≥ Hs·(h0 + x_%s) = %s; lake level %s'
      % (k_av + 1, ON['beat'], ON['mother'], ON['mother'], ON['mother'], fl(wa['x' + ON['mother']] - wa['c' + ON['mother']]), ON['mother'],
         fl(X.XBASE['Hs'] * (X.XBASE['h0'] + wa['x' + ON['mother']])), fl(ON['host'].level(wa))))
s = ON['s']
first_N = s.index('N')
print('  Four classes reported separately (number of events): original path kept %d, new path bears same duty %d, same-kind relation only %d, duty interrupted %d'
      % tuple(s.count(c) for c in 'ONkx'))
print('  Transition period (from the avulsion until the new route first bears the duty): ' + ' '.join('%s[%s]' % (s[i], ON['kind'][i] or '—') for i in range(k_av + 1, first_N)))
print('  New route first bears the duty: event %d; all %d events from then to the window end borne by the new route %s' % (first_N + 1, len(s) - first_N, set(s[first_N:]) == {'N'}))
EVSEQ = [b for b in FORCING if b != 'F']
print('  Three time points of “avulsion” registered separately: new route opens at event %d (%s); new route first takes a majority of the actual inflow at event %d (%s); new route first meets the complete duty at event %d (%s)'
      % (k_av + 1, EVSEQ[k_av], ON['maj'], ON['majev'], first_N + 1, EVSEQ[first_N]))
check('X3f opening, first majority and first complete duty are three different time points: the opening is on a high flow (event 13), the first majority on the following low flow (event 14), the first complete duty at event 26—that the opening happens on a high flow does not license inferring that the majority is also taken on a high flow',
      (k_av + 1, ON['maj'], first_N + 1) == (13, 14, 26) and EVSEQ[k_av] == 'B' and ON['majev'] == 'S')
shN, soN = [], []
for i in range(k_av + 1, first_N):
    for e in ('B', 'S'):
        _sg, tt, qq = ON['host'].terms(ON['states'][i], e)
        shN.append(qq['N'] / ON['host'].ev[e][0])
        soN.append(tt['N']['out'] / sum(tt[j]['out'] for j in tt))
print('  Shares of the new route N during the transition period (both inflow classes): throughflow %s–%s; channel-exit sediment at least %s' % (fl(min(shN)), fl(max(shN)), fl(min(soN))))
check('X3e what the transition period lacks is the throughflow share: the channel-exit sediment share of N is ≥ θ throughout, while its throughflow share is < θ throughout (the sediment-transport duty transfers before the throughflow duty)',
      min(soN) >= TH and max(shN) < TH)
check('X3a the avulsion is an endogenous event: it occurs on a high-flow beat of the feedback-on arm, and its parent route is the original bearer P; before the avulsion P keeps the duty throughout',
      k_av is not None and ON['mother'] == 'P' and set(s[:k_av + 1]) == {'O'})
check('X3b all four classes occur on the same trajectory and are reported separately: original path kept → after the avulsion, a transition period in which only “same-kind relation only” and “duty interrupted” alternate → the new path N bears the same duty and keeps it to the window end',
      all(c in s for c in 'ONkx') and set(s[k_av + 1:first_N]) <= {'k', 'x'} and set(s[first_N:]) == {'N'}
      and all(w == 'N' for w in ON['who'][first_N:]))
we = ON['end']
_s, tE, qE = ON['host'].terms(we, 'B')
print('  Healing of the abandoned route: at the avulsion c_P %s, u_P %s → at the window end c_P %s, u_P %s; at the window end route P takes %s / %s of a high flow'
      % (fl(wa['cP']), fl(wa['uP']), fl(we['cP']), fl(we['uP']), fl(qE['P']), fs(ON['host'].ev['B'][0])))
check('X3c healing of the abandoned route: at the window end the gully-mouth incision of P is shallower than at the avulsion, the in-channel aggradation of the reach (c_P < 0) recovers toward the plain surface (|c_P| decreases), and the share of a high flow taken by P is below θ (registered as failure)',
      we['uP'] < wa['uP'] and wa['cP'] < 0 and abs(we['cP']) < abs(wa['cP']) and qE['P'] < TH * ON['host'].ev['B'][0])
NA = traj({'avulse': False})
print('  Crevassing-disabled arm (feedback on): %s' % NA['s'])
check('X3d necessity reading for the mechanism (on the trajectory tested): with feedback on but crevassing disabled, no new route bears the duty; P loses the duty intermittently in the later part (“same-kind relation only” occurs), and “new path bears same duty” does not occur',
      'N' not in NA['s'] and 'k' in NA['s'] and NA['s'][:k_av + 1] == s[:k_av + 1])
# ============================================================ X4
print('\nX4  Condition table: lake-outlet outflow capacity ko and spillway sill z0 (all else default; same inflow)')
table = {}
for ko, z0 in itertools.product((Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2), Fr(1), Fr(2)), (Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2))):
    r = traj({'ko': ko, 'z0': z0})
    tail = r['s'][-10:]
    table[(ko, z0)] = (r['avul'], tail, r['host'].level(r['end']))
    print('  ko = %s, z0 = %s: avulsion %s; last 10 events %s; final lake level %s' % (fs(ko), fs(z0), 'event %d' % (r['avul'] + 1) if r['avul'] is not None else 'none', tail, fl(table[(ko, z0)][2])))
av = lambda ko, z0: table[(ko, z0)][0]
~~~

~~~text
      and av(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 4)) is not None and av(Fr(1, 4), Fr(1, 2)) <= av(Fr(1, 2), Fr(1, 2)))

              % (fl(a['outP']), fl(a['basin']), fl(a['export']), fl(a['removed']), fl(r['end']['V']), fl(r['host'].level(r['end']))))
cl_all = all(arm[('clamped', nm)][0]['avul'] is None and set(arm[('clamped', nm)][0]['s']) == {'O'} for nm, _p, _r in ARMS)
~~~

A.6  dm21\_run\_law.py  Run entry 3: law-formation read-back (sections L0–L8)

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields v1.0, appendix program: run entry for the law-formation read-back of one candidate relation (model side; local model 𝔐21; dm21_law.py).
Run: python3 dm21_run_law.py (separate entry from dm21_run.py and dm21_run_lake.py)
Sections: L0 frozen declaration; L1 the same criterion checked before and after formation (time point of first occurrence); L2 Established item by item; L3 target and increment contrast of DynamicRole;
    L4 the three segment-level facts of ΩSupport and the class-labeled reading Ω^c; L5 exhaustive verification of way two of SC_Hold (declared current-capacity quantity) on the declared family of histories;
    L5d maintenance-free two-entry cycle (another kind of current capacity); L6 interventions: first occurrence, exit or regaining of the structural positive end for the same R, with water-route relation, re-traversal window and current capacity in separate columns;
    L7 derived readings of the failure condition; L8 direct physical readings at the prediction layer (main-throughflow shares for both inflow classes, complete duty, structural positive end read separately). Scope of signature: the frozen model 𝔐21, the declaration and working domain of main text 8.5, the time points listed.
Each item prints “pass” or “fail”; a summary follows at the end; the exit code is 1 if any item fails. Standard library only; every numeric value is a Fraction; no randomness."""
import os
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as Fr
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
~~~

~~~python
import jhc_core as JH
~~~

~~~python
STAT = dict(nodes=0, ante=0, viol=0)

def dfs(seq, ms):
    t = len(seq)
    if t >= 8:
        STAT['nodes'] += 1
        w = ms[-1]
        if L.established(g, w)[0] and L.omega_support(g, ms, seq, t)[0] and L.dynamic_role(g, w, REF[t])[0]:
            STAT['ante'] += 1
            key = tuple(sorted(w.items()))
            if key not in CAP:
                CAP[key] = G.dom_N(g, w, TH, 2, 3, DIST, 8, 1)[0]
            if CAP[key] != '1':
                STAT['viol'] += 1
    if t == HLEN:
        return
    for c in 'BS':
        dfs(seq + [c], ms + [G.macro(g, ms[-1], c, 3)])

print('\nL5d  Another kind of current capacity: the maintenance-free two-entry cycle from the structural positive end (reported separately from the finite-horizon Cap_8 under perturbation)')

def cycle(w, period, cap=256):
    """From w, alternate according to period (3 rest beats after each event); detect a return using (phase, all non-V positions) as the exact key; at each step read first, then advance.
    Returns ('cycle', step first seen, step seen again) or ('fail', steps continued)."""
    seen = {}
    v = w
    test = 'BS' if len(period) > 1 else 'S'
    for k in range(cap):
        key = (k % len(period), tuple(v[x] for x in g.X if x != 'V'))
        if key in seen:
            return ('cycle', seen[key], k)
        if not all(L.read(g, v, e) for e in test):
            return ('fail', k)
        seen[key] = k
        v = G.macro(g, v, period[k % len(period)], 3)
    return ('undetermined', cap)

CYC = {}
for nm, t_ in (('H1', 10), ('H1m', 15)):
    for per in ('BS', 'SB', 'S'):
        CYC[(nm, per)] = cycle(RD[nm][1][t_], per)
        print('  %s from the end of macro-step %d, sequence %s: %s' % (nm, t_, per + '…' if len(per) > 1 else 'low flows only', CYC[(nm, per)]))
check('L5d maintenance-free cycle: from the end of macro-step 10 of H1, with either initial phase BS or SB, the state without V together with the phase repeats exactly at the ends of continuation macro-steps 27 and 29, and both main relations hold at every read in the prefix and in the cycle; from the end of macro-step 15 of H1m the corresponding values are 22 and 24',
      CYC[('H1', 'BS')] == ('cycle', 27, 29) and CYC[('H1', 'SB')] == ('cycle', 27, 29)
      and CYC[('H1m', 'BS')] == ('cycle', 22, 24) and CYC[('H1m', 'SB')] == ('cycle', 22, 24))

      CYC[('H1', 'S')] == ('fail', 27) and CYC[('H1m', 'S')] == ('fail', 29))
# ============================================================ L6
print('\nL6  Interventions: first occurrence, exit or regaining of the structural positive end (Established ∧ DynamicRole ∧ ΩSupport) for the same R; water-route relation, re-traversal window and current capacity in separate columns')
SEQS = 'B' * 8 + 'SB' * 8
print('  History H★ = (B·R³)⁸(S·R³·B·R³)⁸; the structural positive end first holds at the end of macro-step 10. Trace-Response interventions are applied at the end of macro-step k (after the rest beats of macro-step k, before event k + 1);')
print('  disconnect @ k: on the event beat with t == k in the program (event k + 1, numbering events from 1) the conductance of P is set to 0, from just after the end of macro-step k to the end of that event beat; the reading at the end of macro-step k already counts the zero conductance of this boundary')

def run_int(k, kind, parts=False):
    w = dict(g.init)
    ref = dict(w)
    ms, rs, flw, cnd = [w], [ref], [], []
    for t, e in enumerate(SEQS):
        h = gc if (kind == 'disconnect' and t == k) else g
        flw.append(h.act(w, e)['QP'])
        cnd.append(h.cond(w, 'P'))
        w = G.macro(h, w, e, 3)
        for _ in range(4):
            ref = g.step(ref, 'R')
        if t + 1 == k:
            if kind == 'revert both-position increments':
                w = dict(w, uP=ref['uP'], cP=ref['cP'])
            elif kind == 'revert u_P increment':
                w = dict(w, uP=ref['uP'])
            elif kind == 'revert c_P increment':
                w = dict(w, cP=ref['cP'])
            elif kind == 'erase mouth bar':
                w = dict(w, mP=Fr(0))
        ms.append(w)
        rs.append(ref)
    cnd.append(g.cond(w, 'P'))
    rows, _m, _r = L.law_readout(g, SEQS, ms=ms, rs=rs, flows=flw, conds=cnd)
    if parts == 'rows':
        return rows, ms
    if parts:
        return ''.join(str(r['Est']) for r in rows), ''.join(str(r['Om']) for r in rows)
    return ''.join(str(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']) for r in rows)

base = run_int(-1, 'none')
print('  No intervention: structural positive end %s' % base)
KINDS = ('revert both-position increments', 'revert u_P increment', 'revert c_P increment', 'erase mouth bar', 'disconnect')
KS = (2, 4, 6, 8, 12, 16)
TAB, PARTS, CAPV = {}, {}, {}
CAPN = dict(bad=0)
for kind in KINDS:
    for k in KS:
        rows_k, ms_k = run_int(k, kind, parts='rows')
        s_ = ''.join(str(r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']) for r in rows_k)
        TAB[(kind, k)] = s_
        PARTS[(kind, k)] = (''.join(str(r['Est']) for r in rows_k), ''.join(str(r['Om']) for r in rows_k))
        for t, (r, w) in enumerate(zip(rows_k, ms_k)):
            if t < k or not (r['Est'] & r['DR'] & r['Om']):
                continue
            key = tuple(w[x] for x in g.X if x != 'V')
            if key not in CAPV:
                CAPV[key] = G.dom_N(g, w, TH, 2, 3, DIST, 8, 1)[0]
            CAPN['bad'] += CAPV[key] != '1'
        f_ = s_.find('1')
        if k < 10:
            note = 'structural positive end first: %s' % ('macro-step %d' % f_ if f_ >= 0 else 'does not appear within 25 macro-steps')
        else:
            lost = [t for t in range(k, len(s_)) if s_[t] == '0']
            rec = next((t for t in range(lost[0], len(s_)) if s_[t] == '1'), None) if lost else None
            note = 'structural positive end: %s' % ('no exit' if not lost else 'exits at macro-step %d, %s' % (lost[0], 'regained at macro-step %d' % rec if rec else 'not regained within 25 macro-steps'))
        print('  %s @ macro-step %d: structural positive end %s; water-route relation (Established) %s; re-traversal window (ΩSupport) %s (%s)' % (kind, k, s_, PARTS[(kind, k)][0], PARTS[(kind, k)][1], note))
print('  Current capacity: on the 30 intervention trajectories, the macro-step ends from the intervention time point on at which the structural positive end holds give %d states after deduplication by the projection without V; Cap_8 ≠ 1 at %d of them (default successor protocol P8, no sustained intervention; carry-over of the parent semantics is a separate condition)' % (len(CAPV), CAPN['bad']))

def first(s_):
    return s_.find('1') if '1' in s_ else None

def lost_after(s_, k):
    return any(s_[t] == '0' for t in range(k, len(s_)))

both = all(first(TAB[('revert both-position increments', k)]) is None for k in (2, 4, 6, 8)) and all(lost_after(TAB[('revert both-position increments', k)], k) for k in (12, 16))
~~~
